                                                                                                        Definitions
                                                                                                              釋義

In this report, the following expressions shall have the following meanings     於本報告內，除文義另有所指外，下列詞彙
unless the context indicates otherwise:                                         具有以下涵義：

“Aggregate Trade Balance”                   the total balances of the net trade receivables, net bills receivable and acceptance
                                            bills which are received from the customers to settle their trade debts
「貿易應收總款項」                                   貿易應收賬款淨額、應收票據淨額及承兌票據款項（從客戶收取以結算其貿易
                                            債務）之總額

“AGM”                                       the annual general meeting of the Company or any adjournment thereof
「股東週年大會」                                    本公司股東週年大會或其任何續會

“Articles”                                  the articles of association of the Company, as amended, supplemented, modified
                                            or otherwise adopted from time to time
「章程細則」                                      本公司不時修訂、補充、修改或以其他方式採納的公司章程細則

“Associates”                                has the meaning ascribed to an “associate” under Rule 14A.06(2) of the Listing
                                            Rules, and further includes any company that constitutes a connected subsidiary
                                            of the Company pursuant to Rule 14A.16 of the Listing Rules due to such
                                            associate’s shareholding therein
「聯繫人（含關連附屬公司）」                              具上市規則第 14A.06(2) 條所賦予聯繫人的涵義，且進一步包括根據上市規則
                                            第 14A.16 條構成本公司關連附屬公司之任何公司（由於有關聯繫人於該等關連
                                            附屬公司持股）

“associate(s)”                              has the meaning ascribed thereto under the Listing Rules
「聯繫人」                                       具上市規則所賦予的涵義

“Audit Committee”                           the audit committee of the Company
「審核委員會」                                     本公司的審核委員會

“Auto-finance Services”                     the provision of financing to the end-users and dealers of the Group’s products
                                            for the purpose of purchasing the Group’s vehicles
「汽車金融服務」                                    向本集團產品的終端用戶及經銷商就購買本集團的車輛而提供融資

“Award Share(s)”                            the Shares to be granted under the Share Award Scheme
「激勵股份」                                      根據股份激勵計劃可授予的股份

“Board”                                     the board of Directors
「董事會」                                       董事會

“CAAM”                                      China Association of Automobile Manufacturers
「中國汽車工業協會」                                  中國汽車工業協會

“Chairman”                                  the chairman of the Board
「董事長」                                       董事會主席

“China” or “PRC”                            the People’s Republic of China, and for the purpose of this report, excludes Hong
  or “Chinese Mainland”                     Kong, the Macao Special Administrative Region of the PRC and Taiwan
「中國」或「中國大陸」                                 中華人民共和國（就本報告而言，並不包括香港、澳門特別行政區及台灣）

     The Group
     集團概況

     Business                                                                           業務
     The Group is one of the leading trucks manufacturers in the PRC which              本集團為中國領先卡車製造商之一，專營研
     specializes in the research, development and manufacture of HDTs,                  發及製造重卡、中重卡、輕卡等以及相關主
     medium-heavy duty trucks, LDTs, etc. and related key assemblies, parts and         要總成及零部件。本集團產品廣泛服務於物
     components. The Group’s products widely serve customer groups in various           流、交通運輸和基礎設施建設等不同行業和
     industries and fields such as logistics, transportation and infrastructure         領域的客戶群。
     construction.


     The Group mainly manufactures trucks and also produces key assemblies,             本集團以整車製造為龍頭，並自製發動機、
     parts and components such as engines, cabins, axles, steel frames, gearboxes,      駕駛室、車橋、車架及變速箱等關鍵零部件
     etc. The Group is a truck manufacturer which has its own research and              及總成，是具有自主研發和製造能力、產業
     development and production capability in trucks as well as the complete            鏈完整的卡車製造商。本集團產品不僅在國
     production chain. Our products are not only sold domestically but also exported    內銷售，還向世界其它國家和地區銷售。此
     to other countries and regions in the world. In addition, the Group provides       外，本集團對本集團產品銷售相關人士及中
     financial services to those parties related to the sales of the Group’s products   國重汽集團提供金融服務。
     and to the CNHTC Group.

RISK MANAGEMENT POLICY AND KEY INTERNAL CONTROL MEASURES                             風險管理政策及關鍵內部控制措施
Credit approval process and credit risk assessment policy                            信貸審批流程與信貸風險評估政策
Prior to the granting of financing services to the borrowers, the relevant           在向借款人提供融資服務之前，金融分部相
business units (“Business Unit(s)”) of the Finance Segment will first review the     關業務部門（「業務部門」）將首先審核潛在借
application of the potential borrower, and conduct appropriate pre-loan or pre-      款人之申請，並對潛在借款人及其擔保人進
lease checks on the potential borrower and its guarantor, which involves (a)         行適當貸前或租前審查，包括 (a) 審核潛在
reviewing the financial reports and statements of the potential borrower; and (b)    借款人之財務報告及報表；及 (b) 對潛在借
performing an assessment on the financial condition of the potential borrower        款人及其權益持有人（僅針對實體）之財務狀
and its equity holder(s) (for entities only), such as the type and value of assets   況進行評估，如潛在借款人所擁有資產類型
owned by the potential borrower.                                                     及價值。


Depending on the type and amount of the financing services, the Business Units       根據融資服務種類及金額，業務部門將於考
will assess and decide the necessity and the amount of security/collateral           慮包括但不限於還款歷史、對借款人公開信
for the granting of each loan or lease on a case by case basis considering the       譽查詢之結果、借款人所擁有之資產價值及
factors including but not limited to the repayment history, results of public        地段以及借款人財務狀況等因素後，根據具
credit search towards the borrower, the value and location of the assets owned       體情況評估及決定提供各項貸款或租賃之必
by the borrower and the financial condition of the borrower.                         要性及擔保 / 抵押品金額。


Relevant business approval forms including but not limited to details of the         本集團將編製包括但不限於金額、還款條款
amounts, repayment terms and the applicable interest rate will be prepared           及適用利率等詳情之相關業務審批表格，並
and the senior management of the relevant Business Unit will give final              由相關業務部門之高級管理層就相關申請作
approval in respect of the relevant application and, pursuant to which, the          出最終批准，據此，業務部門將執行相關的
Business Unit will execute the relevant drawdown or payment procedures.              提款或付款程序。


Ongoing monitoring of loan collection and recovery                                   持續監控貸款催收及回收
Various departments of the Business Units (principally engaged in post loan          不同業務部門（主要負責貸後管理）參與監控
management) are involved in monitoring loan repayment and recovery. Such             貸款償還及回收。有關部門至少每季度向風
departments report to the risk management and operations departments on              險管理及營運部門報告所有貸款及融資的償
the repayment status of all loans and financing on at least a quarterly basis        還情況並在發生任何重大違約貸款時立即報
and report any material defaulted loans immediately upon occurrence. In              告。此外，本集團就借款人財務狀況及抵押
addition, the Group carries out regular or specific inspections in respect of the    品狀況進行定期或特定檢查。
financial status of the borrowers and the status of the collaterals.


The Group has also adopted a policy for loan collection/recovery, pursuant to        本集團亦採取貸款催收 / 回收政策，據此，
which, depending on the status of the overdue payment, the Business Units            根據逾期付款的支付情況，業務部門將繼續
will continuously contact the borrower via different means including by phone        通過電話、面談等多種方式與借款人聯絡，
and on-site interviews, issuing overdue payment reminders to the borrower,           向借款人發出逾期付款提醒，並可基於業務
and, based on the approval of the senior management of the Business Units,           部門高級管理層的批准就償還或結清貸款與
the Business Units may negotiate with the borrower for the repayment or              借款人協商。根據上述措施的結果，業務部
settlement of the loan. Depending on the outcome of the aforesaid measures,          門亦可指示其法律顧問發出正式的法律催款
the Business Units may also instruct legal advisers to issue formal legal            函或進行正式法律追索程序。
demand letters or carry out formal legal proceedings for collection of loans.

     Based on the current financial forecast and the funding that can be utilized, the   根據現行財務預測和可動用的融資情況，本
     Group will have sufficient financial resources to continue its operations in the    集團在可預見未來有充足財務資源以持續經
     foreseeable future. As a result, the financial statements were prepared based       營。因此，在編製財務報表時已基於持續經
     on the going concern assumption.                                                    營基準編製。

     DISCLAIMER ON NON-GAAP FINANCIAL MEASURES                                           非公認會計原則指標的免責聲明
     Affiliated HDT export revenue is a non-GAAP financial measure and it is used        聯營重卡出口收入為非公認會計原則指標，
     for assessing the Group’s actual export performance. The Group treats the type      該指標用於評估本集團的實際出口表現。本
     of sales to dealers who purchase the Group’s HDTs and directly export to their      集團將經銷商購買本集團重卡後直接出口至
     overseas customers as our export sales. Hence, it may not be comparable to          其海外客戶的銷售類型視為出口銷售。因
     similarly titled measures for other companies and should not be considered          此，聯營重卡出口收入未必可與其他公司的
     as an alternative to revenue by geographical markets as an indicator of the         同類指標作比較，也不應被視作地區市場收
     operating performance of the Group.                                                 入（本集團業務指標）的替補。


     The use of non-GAAP financial measure is provided solely to enhance                 非公認會計準則指標的提供僅為加強投資者
     the overall understanding of the Group’s current financial performance.             對本集團現時財務表現的整體理解。此外，
     Additionally, since the Group has historically reported non-GAAP results            由於本集團以往也向投資者報告若干非公認
     to investors, it is considered the inclusion of non-GAAP financial measure          會計原則指標以反映本集團業績，因此本集
     provides consistency in the Group’s financial reporting.                            團認為非公認會計原則指標可使本集團的財
                                                                                         務報告保持一致性。

Diversity Policy and Nomination Procedures                                          多元化政策及提名程序
The Company recognizes and fully appreciates the importance of achieving            本公司認同並深知實現董事會成員多元化
diversity in Board membership regarding corporate governance and Board              對企業管治及董事會效能的重要性。本公
effectiveness. The Company adopted a board diversity policy on 1 September          司於 2013 年 9 月 1 日採納董事會成員多元化
2013. The diversity policy aims to ensure that the members of the Board have        政策。多元化政策旨在確保董事會成員在技
an appropriate balance of skills, experience and perspectives, so as to enhance     能、經驗以及視角的多元化方面達到適當的
the effective operation of the Board and maintain high standards of corporate       平衡，從而提升董事會的有效運作，保持高
governance. It also sets measurable objectives for selecting candidates based       標準的企業管治水平。參考本公司的業務模
on a range of diverse perspectives, taking into account the business model and      式和特定需求，基於一系列多元化視角，制
specific needs of the Company, including but not limited to gender, age, race,      定甄選候選人的可量化指標，包括但不限於
language, cultural background, educational background, industry experience          性別、年齡、種族、語言、文化背景、教育
and professional experience.                                                        背景、行業經驗和專業經驗。

following measurable objectives:                                                    化指標：


1.    at least one third of the Directors shall be independent non-executive        1.   至少三分之一董事須為獨立非執行董
      Directors;                                                                         事；


2.    at least one Director and senior management is female; and                    2.   至少一名董事及高級管理層為女性；
                                                                                         及


3.    at least one Director shall have obtained accounting or other professional    3.   至少一名董事須已獲得會計或其他專
      qualifications.                                                                    業資格。


During the Period, all the measurable objectives have been fulfilled.               回顧期內，所有可量化指標均已達成。

Dividend Policy                                                                  股息政策
The Company did not have a dividend policy in place, as the Company will         本公司並未設立股息政策，因為本公司會綜
consider various factors such as the current operating results, distributable    合考慮當時的經營業績、可分派儲備、財務
reserves, financial position, expected financial performance, expected working   狀況、預期財務表現、預期營運資金需求、
capital requirements, sustainable development to determine the dividend          可持續發展等因素以確定股息分配方案，此
distribution proposal, which is to the best interest of the Company and its      舉符合本公司及其股東之整體利益。
Shareholders as a whole.

Introduction                                                                         引言
We have reviewed the interim financial report set out on pages 93 to 144 which       我們已審閱列載於第 93 頁至第 144 頁的中
comprises the consolidated statement of financial position of Sinotruk (Hong         國重汽（香港）有限公司（以下簡稱「貴公司」）
Kong) Limited (the “Company”) as of 30 June 2026 and the related consolidated        的中期財務報告，此中期財務報告包括於
statement of profit or loss, statement of comprehensive income and statement         2026 年 6 月 30 日的綜合財務狀況表，與截
of changes in equity and condensed consolidated cash flow statement for the          至該日止六個月期間的相關綜合損益表、全
six-month period then ended and explanatory notes. The Rules Governing the           面收益表和權益變動表及簡明綜合現金流量
Listing of Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited require the         表，以及附註解釋。香港聯合交易所有限公
preparation of an interim financial report to be in compliance with the relevant     司證券上市規則規定，就中期財務資料擬備
provisions thereof and Hong Kong Accounting Standard 34, Interim financial           的報告必須符合上述規則中有關條文及由香
reporting as issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants.      港會計師公會頒佈的香港會計準則第 34 號
The directors are responsible for the preparation and presentation of this           「中期財務報告」的規定。       貴公司董事須負
interim financial report in accordance with Hong Kong Accounting Standard 34.        責根據《香港會計準則》第 34 號編製及呈報

Scope of review                                                                      審閱範圍

Engagements 2410, Review of interim financial information performed by               準則第 2410 號「實體獨立核數師對中期財務
the independent auditor of the entity as issued by the Hong Kong Institute of        資料的審閱」進行審閱。中期財務報告審閱
Certified Public Accountants. A review of interim financial report consists of       工作包括主要向負責財務會計事項人員作出
making inquiries, primarily of persons responsible for financial and accounting      詢問，並實施分析和其他審閱程序。審閱範
matters, and applying analytical and other review procedures. A review is            圍遠小於根據《香港審計準則》進行之審計，
substantially less in scope than an audit conducted in accordance with Hong          故此我們未能保證可知悉所有在審計中可能

1   BASIS OF PREPARATION (continued)                                            1   編製基準（續）
    This interim financial report contains condensed consolidated financial         本中期財務報告包括簡明綜合財務報
    statements and selected explanatory notes. The notes include an                 表和經挑選的說明附註。該等附註闡

                                         observable inputs which fail to meet Level 1,                      估值： 採 用 的 是 第 二 級
